Like, I stopped caring about keeping track of meals, and then I’d go days without having real food—just coffee and protein bars. I didn’t really notice how bad it got until my clothes started fitting loose.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel anxious or upset when you think about eating a normal meal?\n\nPatient: Not really anxious or upset, no. It’s more that my eating went completely off the rails the other way — I stopped caring about tracking anything, skipped actual meals for days, and just got by on coffee and protein bars. My clothes started hanging loose before I really even noticed.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you might lose control over how much you eat if you start eating normally again?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t really feel like I’d lose control eating normally. The problem has been more that I just stopped paying attention to food at all — I quit tracking, skipped real meals for days, and was basically getting by on coffee and protein bars. My clothes started hanging loose before I even really noticed.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt afraid of gaining weight at any point in your life?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. It wasn’t like I was eating less on purpose because of the weight. I just stopped caring about food tracking, skipped actual meals for days, and was mostly running on coffee and protein bars.
